# GUI-BORDER-01 preregistration and implementation

GUI_BORDER_EXECUTION_STATE: COMPLETE

The preregistered synthetic study ran exactly once. Its report-bound gzip
evidence is `docs/reports/GUI-BORDER-01-evidence-2026-10-05.jsonl.gz`
(122,309,975 bytes; SHA-256
`3b9e82beec101bc8a405c1231a0a019924be7e0523b615c55deb21e9a68b0341`).
Astra independently verified all 58 gzip members, pair seals, artifact digest,
camera-error arithmetic and aggregate counts. The pilot gate FAILED: primary
camera passes improved from 1/12 (A) to 6/12 (B), but the preregistered B
threshold is 9/12. B had 3 wrong emissions and 3 refusals among primary scenes;
there were zero lost A-pass families and zero newly wrong stress emissions.
The treatment is not approved for production integration. No real screenshot,
geometry, block-state, `.litematic`, or product-acceptance result is established;
production calibration and gates remain unchanged.

## Frozen intervention

A delegates the unchanged public RGB -> automatic GUI mask -> zero-filled grey
-> enhancement -> LSD -> camera path. A returns the original detector object.
B delegates precisely the same detector once and returns an ordered copy of a
whole-segment subset. Both arms receive only RGB and a frame identity. No truth,
manual context, HUD coordinates, camera parameters, weights, or axis gates enter
inference. The exact automatic mask returned by that arm's single delegated mask
call supplies the filter; the mask is not recomputed or mutated.

Mark both pixels of every horizontally/vertically adjacent IN-IMAGE mask-value
transition. The image perimeter supplies no boundary. Dilate this mask by a
3-pixel Chebyshev radius (7x7 ones, outside false). For each raw segment, sample
ceil(Euclidean length)+1 equally spaced points including both endpoints. Pixel
lookup is floor(coordinate+0.5); out-of-image samples count as no overlap rather
than being clipped. Exclude the entire segment iff at least half the samples
hit the boundary mask. Zero-length lines have one sample. Preserve original raw
indices, relative order, coordinates and dtype. No redetection, clipping,
residual filter, horizontal/vertical rejection, or scene-specific mask exists.
B can drop below 12 retained segments and thereby invoke the existing
estimator support refusal on the treatment side; this is not a new estimator
rule. The unchanged <12 support refusal remains; weak axes are reported
without adding a veto. Resource/instrumentation failures abort the one-shot
study without a partial performance conclusion or retry.

Both arms compute the diagnostic filter for matching evidence/cost accounting;
A's optimizer still receives every original segment. Report each arm's wall
time plus accepted trace overhead and objective-delegate time separately.
These instrumentation timings are not a production runtime benchmark.

## Fixture registry and denominators

Six previously exposed CAM-MD physical scenes and the exposed INPUT-01
identifiable RGB are DEVELOPMENT only (7). Twelve new primary physical families
are 4 planar, 4 stepped, 4 occluding, each with a frozen PCG64 seed, independent
native16 seeded Fourier RGB, new world geometry, and one of four yaw/pitch/FOV
combinations: (11,-21,52), (38,-9,67), (62,-27,79), (81,-16,61) degrees. Native
primary image shape is 360x480. Each family also supplies fixed native crop,
low-texture, and GUI-only contamination variants (36 correlated stress cases).
Two physical HUD-free/structure-crosses-mask controls complete 57 scene pairs.
Only the 12 unique primary RGBs enter the primary denominator. Crop is the native
subarray [x37,y19,w418,h314] with translated principal point. GUI contamination
changes only the existing automatic GUI mask pixels, and is paired to the
family's primary prediction within EACH arm. Low texture changes appearance
without changing ray-hit geometry. Rendering uses nearest positive finite world
plane/box-face intersections; no screen-space depth compositor is heldout.

Two low-texture stress RGBs (planar-1 and occluding-1) are identical despite
separate geometry recipes. Preflight binds their RGB SHA, camera-truth SHA and
geometry-truth SHA and labels an image-identifiability geometry collision.
Their CAMERA truth is identical, so both camera-stress results remain, with
no independent-observation or unique geometry/block-state recovery claim.
Any equal-RGB group with different camera truth is wholly excluded from camera
accuracy scoring/denominators and explicitly reported. Primary RGB uniqueness
is mandatory. This rule is frozen before any fit; it is not a scoring exception
selected after outcomes. See the exact collision bindings in JSON below.

## Preregistered evaluation and pilot rule

Every full A/B prediction plus accepted call-through trace, raw/mask/retained
byte snapshots, ordered indices, sample counts and hashes must be sealed and
durably retained BEFORE renderer truth is consulted. Pair replay requires equal
RGB bytes/hash/shape, exact masks, enhancement and raw detector outputs. Retained
samples are replayed from the raw indices. Full objective records must use the
retained array and its unchanged Euclidean length weights. Accepted CAM tracing
records candidate_max as highest TESTED candidate, separately from captured
coarse winner -> full rescore, fine winner -> actual refinement acceptance, and
final prediction. No optimizer loops are rewritten.

Diagnostic pass is focal relative error <=5%, orientation geodesic modulo
horizontal quarter-turn <=1 degree, principal error <=1% image diagonal.
Report each emitted and renderer camera's objectives on ORIGINAL and FILTERED
lines separately, each normalized by that set's own total length; never compare
cross-set raw scores. Report residuals/inliers, per-axis support and weak-axis
minimum, camera errors/pass, wrong/refusal, source/retained count and length,
actual stages, runtime and scene-level signed paired differences. Camera
confidence remains uncalibrated. Refusal replacing a wrong emission is a safety
improvement, never accuracy recovery. Stress remains correlated and cannot
increase primary n.

The pilot gate requires B >=9/12 primary passes, B-A >=3 passes, zero lost
A-pass families, zero newly wrong emissions in stress, and GUI-only prediction
stability within each arm. Passing authorizes no integration by itself: it
supports only bounded integration REVIEW plus independent real-screenshot
validation, with product geometry/state/M3 gates unchanged. Equal A/B successes
are reported as such; they are not attributed to the filter.

## Durable one-shot and bounded gzip evidence

The explicit preregistration setup renders/hashes fixtures reproducibly.
Importing this new harness does not freeze/render its 57 fixtures. At execution,
load the reviewed report JSON without rendering, verify source/runtime/registry/
policy pins and its SHA, reject any existing evidence artifact, then lock/fsync
NOT_STARTED -> RUNNING before registered rendering or inference. The accepted
CAM dependency still performs its historical development-only stimulus freeze
on its own import; it performs no fit/metric, and this revision does not alter it.

Evidence is `docs/reports/GUI-BORDER-01-evidence-2026-10-05.jsonl.gz` ONLY, created
exclusively with one open single-owner writer. It is standard concatenated gzip:
one header member followed by one independent member per complete sealed pair.
Each member contains exactly one canonical JSONL row. Stdlib gzip level6,
filename empty, mtime0; finalize the gzip trailer in memory BEFORE artifact
append, flush and fsync. Headers/trailers count towards compressed caps. Never
truncate, overwrite, repair, retry or drop a failed/oversized member. Earlier
complete members survive a later failure; a partially written final member
invalidates the run while retaining the prefix and RUNNING marker.

Cap each compressed member at 16 MiB (16,777,216 bytes), including its header and
CRC/trailer; cap the whole compressed artifact at 512 MiB (536,870,912 bytes).
Retain the 200,000,000-byte decompressed JSONL-row cap per member. A capped sink
checks compressed allocation during encoding; bounded streaming decoding checks
output size as bytes emerge and requires gzip CRC/trailer/eof. Reject truncation,
trailing junk, malformed tails, empty/reordered/additional members, wrong seals,
unknown offsets, payload mismatch and compressed digest mismatch. Appends must
start at the owner's expected offset. Finalize, append, flush/fsync and read back
ONLY the new complete member, checking bytes/digest and bounded decoding against
the preverified full sealed payload; do not rescan earlier prefixes per append.

After ALL 57 pairs are durable, perform ONE bounded full-stream member/payload/
seal replay and compare the compressed artifact digest with the writer's digest
BEFORE the first evaluator/truth access. Evaluation reads already verified
members by their recorded offsets with per-member digest/seal checks. Store only
member descriptors/seals and small predictions, not all trace payloads. After
evaluation, hash compressed bytes once more; any difference forbids COMPLETE.
Only then append the registered result and fsync COMPLETE. Failures/interruption
leave RUNNING plus the append-only prefix and forbid any rerun. Source and total
compressed caps are frozen and checked before registered rendering/inference.

Filter caps stay 10,000 raw segments, 20,001 samples/segment, 50,000,000 sampled
positions. Accepted CAM trace caps stay 120,000 score events, 32 stage events,
2,000,000 array bytes, 64,000,000 event bytes and 10,000 segments per arm. These
are resource ceilings, not an actual file-size, runtime or successful-completion
prediction. Compression/member verification/full replay add IO/CPU outside arm
timing. Caps/storage/trace failure aborts honestly without adaptive enlargement.

## No-study QA and limits

Focused command: PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1, PYTHONPATH=src,
.venv/Scripts/python.exe -B -m pytest tests/test_gui_border.py -o addopts=
-p no:cacheprovider -q --no-header

Final remediated result: 87 passed in 9.82s, exit0 (session67150). Gzip checkpoint
63passed5.72s; expanded79passed/1failed10.85s was tuple-vs-JSON-list expectation
in the new stub COMPLETE assertion, corrected without changing behavior;
86passed9.70s then final frozen-ceiling control87passed9.82s. These tests use
stubs and in-memory evidence; they do not establish numerical study outcomes.
The separate one-shot study result and real sealed evidence are documented below.
All fixture recipes/RGB
hashes/filter/scoring/diagnostic and pilot gates were independently compared
with the prior preregistration and are exactly unchanged.

Tests use pure mask/segment/renderer, camera/detector/metric/objective STUBS,
real public mask/enhancement/trace wrappers and IN-MEMORY report/evidence objects.
Subprocess import/reproducibility checks render stimulus setup only, no fit or
metric. No actual evidence file or other output artifact is created. Tests now
exercise the complete RUNNING->COMPLETE/result-append branch with evaluator and
aggregate stubs, verify one full replay before any evaluator spy and final
compressed digest before COMPLETE/fsync, and prove final-digest veto retains
RUNNING without a result. They include independent gzip concatenation/standard
reader compatibility, trailer/CRC/truncation, junk/empty/extra/reordered members,
same-length earlier-member mutation, wrong seal/digest, short writes and retained
prefix, single-owner/exclusive creation/expected offsets, compressed/decompressed
exact caps and one-less rejection, bounded encode/decode, frozen total ceiling,
explicit reproducible setup and rendering-free execution preload. Actual owned
file AST and byte-whitespace checks are present in the suite and run separately.
The earlier filter/parity/refusal/restoration/partial-install/mutation/pin/gate/
collision controls remain. Calibration exactly matches its prior production pin.
No real optimizer A/B parity, camera performance or outcome-based tuning is
claimed by stub QA. The accepted CAM suite separately covered genuine unchanged
development-image optimizer trace parity.

HUD-free images can contain real world edges at the assumed automatic HUD
boundary. These controls deliberately expose that ambiguity. The filter cannot
identify whether a boundary edge is genuine world structure or a mask artifact;
it never justifies a general line rejection rule or manual HUD coordinates.
Planar/weak-axis scenes remain ambiguous after artifact suppression. All fixtures
were synthetic and the twelve-family pilot is now consumed heldout data. The
observed camera improvement is diagnostic only: the preregistered promotion gate
failed, no production integration is authorized, and no geometry, exact-state,
real-image, causal or product-performance gain is established. Any next camera
study must preserve these families as heldout and use a new untouched evaluation
set.

## Post-run failure analysis (read-only, no refit)

The filter removed 167 of 511 primary segments and 47.12% of their total length. It recovered five new primary families; one prior pass stayed correct. The three refusals (planar-3, stepped-1, stepped-3) retained 5, 2, and 1 segments respectively, below the unchanged 12-segment estimator minimum, and produced zero objective-score events. Changing the loss cannot recover these cases while the support policy remains fixed.

| Family | A → B | Raw → retained | B failure evidence |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Planar-1 | wrong → wrong | 35 → 21 | coarse selection favors wrong camera under current objective |
| Planar-2 | wrong → pass | 34 → 17 | recovered |
| Planar-3 | wrong → refusal | 17 → 5 | below existing support minimum |
| Planar-4 | wrong → wrong | 54 → 42 | coarse selection slightly favors wrong camera under current objective |
| Stepped-1 | wrong → refusal | 18 → 2 | below existing support minimum |
| Stepped-2 | wrong → pass | 65 → 50 | recovered |
| Stepped-3 | wrong → refusal | 13 → 1 | below existing support minimum |
| Stepped-4 | wrong → pass | 50 → 38 | recovered |
| Occluding-1 | wrong → wrong | 47 → 34 | renderer score favors truth; coarse search selects another basin |
| Occluding-2 | pass → pass | 67 → 48 | shared success |
| Occluding-3 | wrong → pass | 32 → 19 | recovered |
| Occluding-4 | wrong → pass | 79 → 67 | recovered |

All nine B camera emissions rejected optical refinement, so remaining wrong estimates originate at coarse selection. On filtered lines, the emitted-vs-renderer normalized objective scores were Planar-1 `.891088` vs `.831110`, Planar-4 `.542547` vs `.536019`, and Occluding-1 `.875140` vs `.883235`. The first two show wrong-camera preference under the current loss; Occluding-1 shows a search-basin miss. Nearest recorded truth-grid candidate vs coarse winner scores were respectively 1236.805/1356.680, 1099.515/1236.392, and 2282.168/2325.952. These are diagnostics on consumed heldout evidence, not a refit or tuning set.

All three wrong cases have zero third-axis support, but Planar-2 passes with the same weak-axis minimum; a blanket axis veto is unsupported. The next candidate experiment is a preregistered angular-residual objective comparison against the existing pixel/length loss, with detector/filter/search/support/gates held fixed, development-only freeze and a new disjoint heldout set. It targets only the wrong-basin cases; support refusals require a separate intervention. Astra recommends GO for this bounded evaluation-only study: freeze \(\tau=\arcsin(6/120)\approx2.866^\circ\) before outcomes and compare both emitted and renderer cameras under both objectives after sealing. Use 12 new physical families with one native-crop and one GUI-only variant each; consumed families remain forensic-only. No objective implementation, new inference, or evidence run has occurred.
